Direct gradient descent details:
Normalize sum=1. Let a (length n nonnegative sum1). Compute c = conv_full(a) (length 2n-1). We want minimize max c. Use softmax weights w_k = exp(λ c_k - max)/Z. Then gradient of L=logsumexp(λ c) wrt a_i = λ * 2 δ_i? where δ_i = ∑_{k} w_k a_{k-i} (convolution of w with a, but only lags valid). Specifically ∂c_k/∂a_i = a_{k-i} if 0≤k-i<n, else 0. So gradient_i = 2λ ∑_k w_k a_{|?} =2λ (w * a reversed?) We can compute via FFT: h_i = ∑_{k=0}^{2n-2} w_k a_{k-i} for i=0..n-1. This is cross-correlation. Can compute by zero padding w and a length L≥3n? Or use relation: c_k = ∑_{j+k?}. For each i, pairs j,i sum k. Let r=k-i. Need a_r. So h_i = ∑_{r=0}^{n-1} a_r w_{i+r}. This is cross-correlation of a and w at lag i. We can compute by FFT: pad a and w to L, h = ifft(fft(a) * fft(w reversed?)). Simpler: define w_rev[m]=w_{M-1?}. But can compute h via np.correlate? For n up to 4096, O(n^2) maybe okay every iter? 16M * hundreds = maybe okay in C? But we can use FFT. Let's derive: We need h_i = ∑_{r=0}^{n-1} a_r w_{i+r}. This is a correlation. Let w_ext length 2n-1. Define b[m]=w_{m}? The autoc? h = np.correlate(w, a, mode='valid')? np.correlate(w, a, 'valid') length len(w)-len(a)+1 = n: output[i]=∑_m w[i+m] a[m]? Yes np.correlate(a, v, mode) computes ∑ a[m+n] v[m]. If use np.correlate(w, a, 'valid'), output[i]=∑_m w[i+m] a[m] =h_i. That is O(n * n)? np.correlate uses direct maybe O(n*m) but in C. FFT: Let L≥3n? We need linear cross-correlation. Define A length L with A[0:n]=a. Define W length L with W[0:2n-1]=w. We want h_i =∑_r a_r w_{i+r}. This is cross-correlation, equals convolution of a with reversed w? Let wr[r]=w_{M-1-r} with M=2n-1? Then (a * wr)_i =∑_r a_r wr[i-r]=∑ a_r w_{M-1-(i-r)} = w_{M-1-i+r}; not. Instead use w_forward, h_i =∑ a_r w_{i+r}. This is (reverse a) * w? Let arev[r]=a_{n-1-r}. Convolution c_i=∑_r arev[r] w[i-r] =∑_{r=0}^{n-1} a_{n-1-r} w_{i-r}. Let m=n-1-r => r=n-1-m: ∑_{m=0}^{n-1} a_m w_{i-(n-1-m)}=∑ a_m w_{i-n+1+m}. We want w_{i+r} with r=m, so offset: if use w shifted by n-1. Define Wshift[t]=w_{t-(n-1)} for t=0..3n-3? Then convolution arev * Wshift at i? Maybe easier: h_i = ∑_{m=0}^{n-1} a_m w_{i+m}. Let define Wpadded index p=i+m. This is like sliding dot product. Cyclic convolution? conj(A) * W in freq corresponds to correlation: r_i = ∑_m A_m W_{i+m} (mod L) if L large enough to avoid wrap. Indeed if L ≥3n-2, nonzero. So h = ifft(conj(fft(a,L)) * fft(w,L)). Gradient descent with sum constraint: We can update a ← a - η (grad - mean of grad? to preserve sum) due to scale invariance? If we fix sum=1 by normalizing after update, sum drift okay. To avoid zero/negative, use multiplicative update or projected gradient with clipping.

Multiplicative update based on gradient: For maximizing sum under max constraints? Could do a_i ← a_i exp(-η * normalized_grad_i) then clip/renorm. Because gradient positive (w nonneg, a nonneg). This preserves nonneg. If gradient high at elements contributing to max lags, reduce them. This is like exponentiated gradient. May work.

Alternatively use subgradient of max: pick active lags (c≥M*0.999), weights uniform. Gradient = sum active a_{k-i}. Now to reduce max, we could solve least squares direction.

But simple gradient may be slow. We can use L-BFGS-B via scipy on smooth objective J= logsumexp(λ c)/λ + penalty for sum? L-BFGS uses many gradient evaluations; each O(L log L). Parametric Fourier optimization could be more effective: Because continuous optimum may be low-dimensional. But direct local search can adapt.

Could use "active set" linear programming idea from previous state-of-art but from good init. The LP: maximize sum g subject to (f*g)_k ≤ M for active k, g≥0. This is linear in g if f fixed! Indeed for a fixed current f and target bound M (e.g. current max convolution), the constraints are linear in g: ∑_i f_i g_{k-i} ≤ M for selected k. Objective ∑g. Solve LP. Then rescale g to sum=1, update f←(1-t)f+t g. This can move far. LP dimension n, constraints active lags maybe moderate. We can use scipy.optimize.linprog? For n=4096 and constraints maybe 100-1000, okay? But linear_program with highdim may be heavy. We can avoid explicit LP by solving a simpler system: if all active constraints equality, find g that increases sum while keeping f*g≤M? The LP dual might give update. But maybe we can implement LP with scipy.optimize.linprog method='highs' (available) for moderate n (e.g. 512) to get good pattern, then scale up? Let's examine LP step more. Suppose current f normalized sum=1. M=max(f*f). We want a new g with sum as large as possible (then normalized sum1) under f*g ≤ M (active constraints). Implement full-dim local optimizer:

- For each iteration:
  - compute c = conv_full_fft(a).real, S = a.sum()

If we multiply all by similar, renorm removes global. Use g_sub = h - ∑(h*a)/∑a? The part that preserves energy? Since renorm removes global, subtracting weighted average maybe stable. We can update log a: loga -= η * g_sub. Then clip a? In log domain, enforce min floor. Start loga finite. We can maintain log_a. Then a=exp(loga-meanloga) normalized (scale invariant). Update loga. This ensures positive and sum. Learning rate can be larger, e.g. 0.1 to 1. Need avoid diverging.

Let's design final program architecture:

```
import numpy as np, time, math

def next_fast_len(m): return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()

def seq_score_fft(a, L=None):
    n=a.size
    if L is None: L=next_fast_len(2*n-1)
    fa=np.fft.fft(a,n=L)
    c=np.fft.ifft(fa*fa).real[:2*n-1]
    # due roundoff, c may have tiny negative; max with 0
    S=a.sum()
    if S<=1e-12: return np.inf
    return 2*n*c.max()/ (S*S)
```
But for score we need nonnegative a. c.max might be slightly negative if all zero; not.

Use `a.astype(np.float64)`.

- We can keep best sequence and score.
- Use adaptive learning rate `lr = 0.1`, `best_score`, `stagnation`.
- Loop while time_left > 0.
   - compute c, M. score=2nM (since sum1). Actually if sum normalized 1, S=1. But due renorm exactly. Use score=2*n*M.

   - h = cross_corr(a,w)
   - g = h - np.dot(a,h)

   - if max_g < 1e-14: increase p or add random.

- update with current lr/p
- compute new score via conv in next loop; if worse than old, revert and reduce lr; else accept.
This uses one conv per iteration plus maybe gradient conv (c) for old. Actually to compute gradient we need c old (conv). Next iteration computes c new and score; we already have. So loop: at start have a, c, score, M? We can compute gradient from current c. Then update. Next iteration recompute c. If new score worse, revert to old a (and maybe old c known?) and adjust. This is okay.

But if we always update, loga may drift far before score evaluated? We evaluate at next loop start. Fine.

Pseudo:
```
a = normalize; loga=log; score, c, M = eval_with_c(a)
best=a.copy,best_score=score
lr=0.2; p=10; step_count=0; bad=0
while time:
   # gradient from current c,M
   if M<=eps: break
   x=c/M; w=power(x,p); normalize
   h=cross(a,w); g=h-dot(a,h); gnorm=maxabs
   if gnorm < 1e-12:
       p=min(p*2,5000); add small noise; continue
   new_log = loga - lr*g/gnorm
   # clip loga relative: new_log -= new_log.max(); floor
   a_new=exp_clip(new_log)
   score_new, c_new, M_new = eval_with_c(a_new)
   if score_new < score - 1e-12:
       accept: loga=a_new etc; if score_new<best update; bad=0; lr=min(lr*1.05,1.0)
   else:
       # revert; choose smaller lr or different p
       bad+=1
       lr = max(lr*0.5, 0.01)
       p=min(p*1.1,5000)
       if bad>30:
           # perturb best and reset
           a = best.copy(); loga=log(a); score,c,M=eval(best); lr=0.5; p=maybe high
           # add low-freq noise
   p = min(p*1.01,1000) # anneal
```
Computing eval_with_c returns c for next gradient. Good.

```
_DE_N=1
def de_objective_small(x):
    n=_DE_N
    a=np.asarray(x,float)
    a=np.clip(a,0,None)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<1e-12: return 1e9
    a=a/s
    c=np.convolve(a,a,'full')
    return 2.0*n*c.max()
```
This is top-level.

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    start=time.monotonic()
    deadline=start+max(0,budget_s-5)
    best_seq=None; best_score=np.inf
    # try small n quick
    if time<deadline: small_n_search(rng, time_budget=min(120, remaining*0.1), update best)
    # try large n
    large_n_search(rng, deadline, update best)
    # final verify best_seq with evaluate_sequence? if time and len not huge
    return [float(x) for x in best_seq] if best_seq is not None else [1.0]
```

Function `score_and_conv_fft(a, L=None)`:
```
a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
n=a.size
if L is None: L=next_fast_len(2*n-1)
fa=np.fft.fft(a,n=L)
c=np.fft.ifft(fa*fa).real
c=c[:2*n-1]
S=a.sum()
if S <= 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(S): return np.inf, c
return float(2.0*n*np.max(c)/(S*S)), c
```
If a has tiny negative due numerical, clip? Before calling ensure a≥0.

`cross_corr(a,w,L)`:
```
fa=np.fft.fft(a,n=L); fw=np.fft.fft(w,n=L)
h=np.fft.ifft(fa*np.conj(fw)).real[:a.size]
# due roundoff maybe slightly negative; but h can be negative? w,a nonneg => h nonneg. Clip small neg to0.
h = np.where(h>0, h, 0)
return h
```

`normalize_pos(a, floor=1e-12)`:
```
a=np.asarray(a,float)
a=np.clip(a, floor, None)
s=a.sum()
if s<=0: return np.full_like(a,1.0/a.size)
return a/s
```

`log_from_a(a, floor)`: log(clip(a,floor,1)) maybe. Since a normalized sum1, max ≤1, log<=0. Use `np.log(np.clip(a, floor, 1.0))`. Then constant zero? We can subtract max (0) not needed.

`a_from_log(loga, floor)`:
```
loga = np.asarray(loga,float)
# to avoid overflow, subtract max
m=loga.max()
a=np.exp(loga-m)
a=np.clip(a, floor, None)
s=a.sum()
if s<=0: a=np.full(a.size,1.0/a.size)
else: a=a/s
return a
```
Since loga-m max 1, sum finite. Constant shift irrelevant after normalization.

Local search details code:
```
def local_search(a, max_seconds, rng=None, p_init=8.0, lr_init=0.25, min_p=1.0, max_p=10000.0):
    a = normalize_pos(a)
    n = a.size
    L = next_fast_len(2*n-1)
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max_seconds
    loga = np.log(np.clip(a, 1e-12, 1.0))
    score, c = score_and_conv_fft(a, L)
    best_a = a.copy(); best_score = score
    p = float(p_init); lr = float(lr_init)
    bad = 0; iter_count=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        M = np.max(c)
        if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 1e-300: break
        # weights
        # Use power p; if p<1, x^p for x=0 OK. p float.
        x = c / M
        x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)
        if p < 16:
            w = np.power(x, p)
        else:
            # log power to avoid under/over? x^p underflow to 0 fine. But if p large and x close 1, okay.
            w = np.power(x, p)
        sw = w.sum()
        if sw <= 0:
            # fallback max index
            w = np.zeros_like(c); w[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0
        else: w /= sw
        h = cross_corr(a, w, L)
        g = h - float(np.dot(a, h))
        gmax = np.max(np.abs(g))
        if not np.isfinite(gmax) or gmax < 1e-14:
            # increase p and possibly add noise
            p = min(max_p, p*2.0)
            if rng is not None:
                # add small log noise
                noise = rng.normal(0, 0.01, n)
                loga += noise - (a@noise)  # keep approx sum?
            bad += 1
            if bad > 20:
                # restart from best with noise
                a = best_a.copy()
                loga = np.log(np.clip(a,1e-12,1.0))
                if rng is not None:
                    K=max(1,n//16)
                    freq=np.arange(1,K+1)
                    ccoeff=rng.normal(0,0.2,np.size(freq)/2?) ...
                p = min(max_p, max(p,5.0)); lr=0.5; bad=0
                score,c=score_and_conv_fft(a,L)
            continue
        # try step
        # update loga
        step = lr * g / gmax
        new_log = loga - step
        # renormalize loga scale to avoid drift: subtract median or max? If subtract max, values after exp scale changed but normalization removes.
        new_log = new_log - float(np.max(new_log))
        new_a = np.exp(new_log)
        new_a = np.clip(new_a, 1e-12, None)
        s = new_a.sum()
        if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s):
            lr = max(0.01, lr*0.5); bad +=1; continue
        new_a /= s
        new_score, new_c = score_and_conv_fft(new_a, L)
        iter_count +=1
        if np.isfinite(new_score) and new_score < score - 1e-10:
            # accept
            loga = new_log - float(np.max(new_log)) # keep bounded
            a = new_a; c = new_c; score = new_score
            if score < best_score:
                best_score = score; best_a = a.copy()
            bad = 0
            lr = min(1.0, lr*1.05)
        else:
            bad +=1
            lr = max(0.01, lr*0.7)
            p = min(max_p, p*1.05)
            if bad > 40:
                # escape: perturb best and reset current to best plus noise
                a = best_a.copy()
                if rng is not None:
                    sigma = 0.2 + 0.2*rng.random()
                    K = max(1, n//8)
                    # create Fourier noise
                    log_a = np.log(np.clip(a,1e-12,1.0))
                    noise = np.sin(2*np.pi*np.arange(n)*rng.integers(2, n//2) / n) * sigma
                    # maybe add random modes
                    log_a += noise
                else:
                    log_a = np.log(np.clip(a,1e-12,1.0))
                a = a_from_log(log_a, 1e-12)
                score,c=score_and_conv_fft(a,L)
                loga=np.log(np.clip(a,1e-12,1.0))
                bad=0; lr=0.8; p=min(max_p,max(p,20.0))
        # anneal p slowly if lore? Optionally:
        if iter_count % 50 ==0:
            p = min(max_p, p*1.05)
    return best_a, best_score
```

Need implement fourier noise top-level helper `add_random_fourier_noise(loga, rng, sigma, K)`. But simpler inside local_search? Top-level no closures, can define top-level `random_fourier_noise(n, rng, sigma, max_k)`.

```
def random_fourier_noise(n, rng, sigma, max_k):
    noise = np.zeros(n)
    if max_k <=0: return noise
    k = np.arange(1, max_k+1)
    amp = sigma / np.sqrt(np.arange(1, max_k+1) + 1.0)
    cr = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, max_k)
    sr = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, max_k)
    angles = 2*np.pi*np.outer(k, np.arange(n))/n
    # To avoid constructing outer full maybe loop or use vector: angles shape (K,n), okay K<=n/8, n=4096 => 512*4096=2M, fine but memory 16MB. Could do inverse FFT: spectrum = zeros; spec[k]=cr+1j sr*amp? Simpler.
    spec = np.zeros(n, complex)
    spec[k] = amp*(cr + 1j*sr)
    spec[-k] = np.conj(spec[k])  # if n length and k < n//2
    noise = np.fft.ifft(spec).real
    noise -= np.mean(noise)
    return noise
```

def fourier_noise(n, rng, sigma, max_k):
    max_k = min(max_k, n//2 -1)
    if max_k<=0: return np.zeros(n)
    k = np.arange(1, max_k+1)
    amp = sigma / np.sqrt(k.astype(float))
    cr = rng.normal(0,1,max_k)
    sr = rng.normal(0,1,max_k)
    spec = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.complex128)
    spec[k] = cr*amp + 1j*sr*amp
    spec[-k] = np.conj(cr*amp + 1j*sr*amp?) Need assign correctly.
```

```
import numpy as np
import time
import math
try:
    from scipy.optimize import differential_evolution
except Exception:
    differential_evolution = None

_DE_N = 1

def de_objective_small(x):
    n = _DE_N
    a = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    s = a.sum()
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return 1e9
    a = a / s
    # direct conv for small n
    c = np.convolve(a, a, 'full')
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if m < 0: m=0
    return float(2.0 * n * m)
```

`next_fast_len`:
```
def next_fast_len(m):
    if m <=1: return 1
    return 1 << (int(m)-1).bit_length()
```
In Python, `(m-1).bit_length()` works for int.

`score_and_conv_fft(a, L=None)`:
```
def score_and_conv_fft(a, L=None):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.inf, np.array([])
    if L is None: L=next_fast_len(2*n-1)
    fa=np.fft.fft(a,n=L)
    c=np.fft.ifft(fa*fa).real
    c=c[:2*n-1]
    S=float(a.sum())
    if S <= 1e-12 or not math.isfinite(S):
        return np.inf, c
    m=float(np.max(c))
    if m < 0 or not math.isfinite(m):
        return np.inf, c
    return float(2.0*n*m/(S*S)), c
```
Use `math.isfinite`.

`cross_corr` as above.

`normalize_pos`:
```
def normalize_pos(a, floor=1e-12):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size==0: return a
    a=np.clip(a, floor, None)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0 or not math.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(a.size, 1.0/a.size)
    return a/s
```

`a_from_log` as above.

`random_fourier_noise`:
```
def random_fourier_noise(n, rng, sigma, max_k):
    if n <=2: return np.zeros(n)
    max_k = min(int(max_k), n//2 -1)
    if max_k <=0: return np.zeros(n)
    k = np.arange(1, max_k+1, dtype=np.int64)
    amp = sigma / np.sqrt(k.astype(np.float64))
    vals = amp * (rng.normal(0.0,1.0,max_k) + 1j*rng.normal(0.0,1.0,max_k))
    spec = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.complex128)
    spec[k] = vals
    spec[-k] = np.conj(vals)
    noise = np.fft.ifft(spec).real
    noise -= float(noise.mean())
    return noise
```
But `rng.normal` with complex? We do separate. `spec[-k] = np.conj(vals)` if k maybe includes n//2? restricted. Good.

`make_sinusoidal`:
```
def make_sinusoidal(n, alpha, phase):
    i=np.arange(n,dtype=np.float64)
    a=1.0 + alpha*np.sin(2.0*math.pi*i/float(n) + phase)
    return normalize_pos(a)
```

`make_fourier_start`:
```
def make_fourier_start(n,K,eps,rng):
    h=random_fourier_noise(n,rng,1.0,K)
    h-=h.mean()
    m=np.max(np.abs(h))
    if m>0: h=h/m
    else: h=np.zeros(n)
    a=1.0+eps*h
    return normalize_pos(a)
```
If h max=1, eps can be up to 1 to ensure nonneg? h min -1, a min 0. For random Fourier normalized by max, min may be -1, max 1. Good. If eps>1, clip. Use eps uniform 0.5-1.0.

def make_gram_start(n, coeffs): # coeffs list of (mode, amp, phase)

```
def make_modes_start(n, modes, base=1.0):
    i=np.arange(n); a=np.full(n, base)
    for k,amp,phase in modes:
       if k==0: a+=amp
       else: a+=amp*np.sin(2π*k*i/n + phase)
    return normalize_pos(a)
```

def large_n_search(max_seconds, rng, current_best_score):

Let's write local_search carefully with no variables undefined.

```
def local_search(a, max_seconds, rng=None, p_init=16.0, lr_init=0.35, max_p=20000.0):
    a = normalize_pos(a, 1e-12)
    n = a.size
    if n==0: return a, np.inf
    L = next_fast_len(2*n-1)
    score, c = score_and_conv_fft(a, L)
    if not math.isfinite(score):
        # fallback
        a = make_sinusoidal(n,1.0,0.0) if n>1 else np.array([1.0])
        score,c=score_and_conv_fft(a,L)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_score=float(score)
    if rng is None: rng = np.random.default_rng(12345)
    loga = np.log(np.clip(a, 1e-12, 1.0))
    p=float(p_init); lr=float(lr_init)
    bad=0; it=0
    while time.monotonic() < time.monotonic? Need store start.
```
Use `start_local=time.monotonic(); deadline_local=start_local+max(1.0,max_seconds)`.

Loop:
```
    while time.monotonic() < deadline_local:
        M = float(np.max(c))
        if M <= 1e-300 or not math.isfinite(M): break
        x = c / M
        x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)
        # if x all zero? no
        # To avoid p=0.
        w = np.power(x, p)
        ws = float(w.sum())
        if ws <= 1e-300:
            idx=int(np.argmax(c)); w=np.zeros_like(c); w[idx]=1.0
        else: w /= ws
        h = cross_corr(a, w, L)
        # h nonnegative but maybe small neg
        h = np.where(h > 0.0, h, 0.0)
        g = h - float(np.dot(a, h))
        gmax = float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
        if gmax < 1e-14 or not math.isfinite(gmax):
            p = min(max_p, p*2.0)
            bad +=1
            if bad > 25:
                # reset from best with noise
                loga = np.log(np.clip(best_a,1e-12,1.0))
                loga = loga + random_fourier_noise(n,rng,0.4,max(1,n//16))
                a = a_from_log(loga,1e-12)
                loga -= float(np.max(loga))
                score,c=score_and_conv_fft(a,L)
                if score < best_score: best_score=score; best_a=a.copy()
                bad=0; lr=0.5; p=min(max_p, max(p,32.0))
            continue
        # update
        new_log = loga - (lr * g / gmax)
        new_log -= float(np.max(new_log))
        new_a = np.exp(new_log)
        # if any nonfinite, fallback smaller step
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(new_a)):
            lr = max(0.01, lr*0.5); continue
        new_a = np.where(new_a < 1e-12, 1e-12, new_a)
        snew = float(new_a.sum())
        if snew <= 0 or not math.isfinite(snew):
            lr = max(0.01, lr*0.5); continue
        new_a /= snew
        new_score, new_c = score_and_conv_fft(new_a, L)
        it +=1
        if math.isfinite(new_score) and new_score < score - 1e-10:
            loga = new_log - float(np.max(new_log))
            a = new_a; c = new_c; score = float(new_score)
            if score < best_score:
                best_score=score; best_a=a.copy()
                print(f"  local update: n={n}, score={best_score:.6f}", flush=True)
            bad=0
            lr = min(1.0, lr*1.08)
        else:
            bad +=1
            lr = max(0.01, lr*0.75)
            p = min(max_p, p*1.08)
            if bad > 50:
                # accept a noisy current? Maybe add noise and continue
                loga = loga + random_fourier_noise(n,rng,0.2,max(1,n//16))
                a = a_from_log(loga,1e-12)
                loga -= float(np.max(loga))
                score,c=score_and_conv_fft(a,L)
                if score < best_score:
                    best_score=score; best_a=a.copy()
                bad=0
                lr=0.5
        # if long time no best improvement? track? We have bad only rejects. It may improve rarely.
    return best_a, float(best_score)
```

`small_n_search` code details:
```
def small_n_search(max_seconds, rng):
    start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+max(1.0,max_seconds)
    best_seq=None; best_score=np.inf
    n_values = [2,3,4,5,6,7,8,9,10,12]
    if max_seconds < 60: n_values = [2,3,4,5,6,8,10]
    if max_seconds < 30: n_values = [2,3,4,5,6]
    for n in n_values:
       if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
       # random restarts
       n_restarts = 4 if n<=6 else 2
       for r in range(n_restarts):
          # random positive
          a = normalize_pos(rng.random(n) + 0.1)
          # also structured: sinusoid for n
          # maybe make_sinusoidal n alpha1 phase random
          if r == 1:
             a = make_sinusoidal(n, 1.0, float(rng.uniform(0,2π)))
          elif r == 2:
             a = make_fourier_start(n, max(1,n//2), float(rng.uniform(0.3,1.0)), rng)
          slice_s = min(3.0, max(0.5, (deadline-time.monotonic())/max(1,(len(n_values)-idx+n_restarts))))? Simpler: 1s per start
          a,sc = local_search(a, 1.5, rng, p_init=8.0, lr_init=0.5)
          if sc<best_score: update
       # DE
       if differential_evolution is not None and deadline-time.monotonic() > 10:
          _DE_N = n
          bounds = [(0.0, 1.0) for _ in range(n)]
          seed_int = int(rng.integers(0, 2**31 - 1))
          try:
             res = differential_evolution(de_objective_small, bounds, maxiter=60, popsize=10, tol=1e-5, seed=seed_int, polish=False, updating='inplace', workers=1)
             if res is not None:
                x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
                a = normalize_pos(x)
                # local polish
                slice_s = min(4.0, max(0.5, (deadline-time.monotonic())/max(1, len(n_values))))
                a,sc = local_search(a, slice_s, rng, p_init=8.0, lr_init=0.5)
                if sc<best_score: update
          except Exception:
             pass
    return best_seq, best_score
```

Let's define `score_conv_fft_with_fa(a,L)` top-level:
```
def score_conv_fft_with_fa(a, L):
    n=a.size
    fa=np.fft.fft(a,n=L)
    c=np.fft.ifft(fa*fa).real
    c=c[:2*n-1]
    S=float(a.sum())
    if S <=1e-12: return np.inf,c,fa
    m=float(np.max(c))
    if m<0 or not math.isfinite(m): return np.inf,c,fa
    return float(2.0*n*m/(S*S)), c, fa
```
For local, S=1.

`score_and_conv_fft(a,L=None)` can use this if needed.

```
def make_modes_start(n, modes):
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for mode in modes:
       k = int(mode[0]); amp = float(mode[1]); phase=float(mode[2])
       if k == 0:
          a += amp
       else:
          a += amp * np.sin(2.0*math.pi*float(k)*i/float(n) + phase)
    return normalize_pos(a, 1e-12)
```

def resample_sequence(a, new_n):
    old = np.asarray(a); n=old.size
    x_old=np.linspace(0,1,n,endpoint=False); x_new=np.linspace(0,1,new_n,endpoint=False)
    b=np.interp(x_new, x_old, old) # but periodic? Use periodic interp maybe.
    return normalize_pos(b)
```

```
import numpy as np
import time
import math
try:
    from scipy.optimize import differential_evolution
except Exception:
    differential_evolution = None

_DE_N = 1

def de_objective_small(x):
    n=_DE_N
    a=np.asarray(x,dtype=np.float64)
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,None)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return 1e9
    a=a/s
    c=np.convolve(a,a,'full')
    m=float(np.max(c))
    if m < 0.0 or not math.isfinite(m):
        return 1e9
    return float(2.0*n*m)

def next_fast_len(m):
    if m <= 1: return 1
    return 1 << (int(m)-1).bit_length()

def normalize_pos(a, floor=1e-12):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size ==0: return a
    a=np.clip(a,floor,None)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s <=0.0 or not math.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(a.size, 1.0/float(a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return a/s

def score_conv_fft_with_fa(a,L):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    n=int(a.size)
    if n==0:
       return np.inf, np.array([],dtype=np.float64), np.array([],dtype=np.complex128)
    fa=np.fft.fft(a,n=L)
    c=np.fft.ifft(fa*fa).real
    c=c[:2*n-1]
    S=float(a.sum())
    if S <= 1e-12 or not math.isfinite(S):
       return np.inf,c,fa
    m=float(np.max(c))
    if m < 0.0 or not math.isfinite(m):
       return np.inf,c,fa
    return float(2.0*float(n)*m/(S*S)), c, fa

def score_and_conv_fft(a, L=None):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    n=int(a.size)
    if n==0: return np.inf, np.array([],dtype=np.float64)
    if L is None: L=next_fast_len(2*n-1)
    s,c,fa=score_conv_fft_with_fa(a,L)
    return s,c

def a_from_log(loga, floor=1e-12):
    loga=np.asarray(loga,dtype=np.float64)
    if loga.size==0: return loga
    m=float(np.max(loga))
    if not math.isfinite(m):
       return np.full(loga.size, 1.0/float(loga.size), dtype=np.float64)
    a=np.exp(loga-m)
    a=np.where(a < floor, floor, a)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s <=0.0 or not math.isfinite(s):
       return np.full(loga.size, 1.0/float(loga.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return a/s
```
Use `np.where(a < floor, floor, a)`; if a has nan, condition false? nan < floor false, leaves nan. But loga finite? If loga has -inf, exp 0; finite. If loga nan, max nan handled earlier. Good.

`random_fourier_noise`:
```
def random_fourier_noise(n, rng, sigma, max_k):
    n=int(n)
    if n <=2 or sigma <=0.0:
        return np.zeros(n,dtype=np.float64)
    max_k=int(max_k)
    max_k=min(max_k, n//2 - 1)
    if max_k <=0:
        return np.zeros(n,dtype=np.float64)
    k=np.arange(1,max_k+1,dtype=np.int64)
    amp=sigma/np.sqrt(k.astype(np.float64))
    cr=rng.normal(0.0,1.0,max_k)
    sr=rng.normal(0.0,1.0,max_k)
    vals=amp*(cr+1j*sr)
    spec=np.zeros(n,dtype=np.complex128)
    spec[k]=vals
    spec[-k]=np.conj(vals)
    noise=np.fft.ifft(spec).real
    noise = noise - float(np.mean(noise))
    return noise
```
If max_k includes k=0? no. If n even and max_k n//2-1 avoids Nyquist. Good.

`make_sinusoidal`, `make_modes_start`, `make_fourier_start` as above.

def local_search(a, max_seconds, rng=None, p_init=16.0, lr_init=0.35, max_p=20000.0):
    a=normalize_pos(a,1e-12)
    n=int(a.size)
    if n==0:
       return np.array([],dtype=np.float64), np.inf
    L=next_fast_len(2*n-1)
    score,c,fa=score_conv_fft_with_fa(a,L)
    if not math.isfinite(score):
       a=make_sinusoidal(n,1.0,0.0)
       score,c,fa=score_conv_fft_with_fa(a,L)
       if not math.isfinite(score):
          a=np.full(n,1.0/float(n)); score,c,fa=score_conv_fft_with_fa(a,L)
    best_a=a.copy()
    best_score=float(score)
    if rng is None:
       rng=np.random.default_rng(54321)
    loga=np.log(np.clip(a,1e-12,1.0))
    p=float(p_init); lr=float(lr_init)
    bad=0; it=0
    start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+max(0.1,float(max_seconds))
    print(...?)
```

new_score=2*n*m_new  # sum=1

S_new=float(new_a.sum())

small_n_search:
```
def small_n_search(max_seconds, rng):
    start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+max(1.0,float(max_seconds))
    best_seq=None; best_score=np.inf
    if max_seconds < 30: n_values=(2,3,4,5,6)
    elif max_seconds < 90: n_values=(2,3,4,5,6,7,8,10)
    else: n_values=(2,3,4,5,6,7,8,9,10,12)
    for idx,n in enumerate(n_values):
       if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
       # random restarts
       n_restart = 3 if n<=6 else 2
       for r in range(n_restart):
           if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
           if r ==0:
              a=normalize_pos(0.1 + rng.random(n))
           elif r ==1:
              a=make_sinusoidal(n,1.0,float(rng.uniform(0,2*math.pi)))
           else:
              a=make_fourier_start(n,max(1,n//2),float(rng.uniform(0.4,1.0)),rng)
           slice_s=min(2.0, max(0.3,(deadline-time.monotonic())/max(1, 2)))
           a,sc=local_search(a, slice_s, rng, p_init=8.0, lr_init=0.6)
           if math.isfinite(sc) and sc<best_score:
              best_score=sc; best_seq=a.copy(); print
       # DE
       if differential_evolution is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 5:
          _DE_N=n
          bounds=[]
          for i in range(n): bounds.append((0.0,1.0))
          seed_int=int(rng.integers(0, 2**31 -1))
          try:
             res=differential_evolution(de_objective_small, bounds, maxiter=80, popsize=12, tol=1e-5, seed=seed_int, polish=False, updating='inplace', workers=1)
          except TypeError:
             try:
                res=differential_evolution(de_objective_small, bounds, maxiter=80, popsize=12, tol=1e-5, seed=seed_int, polish=False)
             except Exception:
                res=None
          except Exception:
             res=None
          if res is not None:
             x=np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
             a=normalize_pos(x,1e-12)
             slice_s=min(4.0, max(0.5, (deadline-time.monotonic())/max(1, len(n_values))))
             a,sc=local_search(a, slice_s, rng, p_init=8.0, lr_init=0.6)
             if math.isfinite(sc) and sc<best_score:
                best_score=sc; best_seq=a.copy(); print
    return best_seq,best_score
```
We need set `global _DE_N` in small_n_search before assigning? In Python, assigning `_DE_N = n` inside function makes it local unless declared global. Need `global _DE_N` at top of small_n_search. Also de_objective_small reads global; if not declared, assignment local won't affect global. So add `global _DE_N`.

Similarly if any other global? no.

Time for DE: `bounds` list of tuples. Good.

def large_n_search(max_seconds, rng, n_pref=0):
    start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+max(1.0,float(max_seconds))
    best_seq=None; best_score=np.inf
    if n_pref >0: n=int(n_pref)
    elif max_seconds >= 800: n=4096
    elif max_seconds >= 350: n=2048
    else: n=1024
    # maybe cap n to 8192
    if n > 8192: n=8192
    L=next_fast_len(2*n-1)
    starts=[]
    phases = np.linspace(0.0,2.0*math.pi,8,endpoint=False)
    for phase in phases:
       starts.append(make_sinusoidal(n,1.0,float(phase)))
    for phase in phases[::2]:
       starts.append(make_sinusoidal(n,0.8,float(phase)))
    for K in (4,16,64,256):
       for _ in range(2):
          starts.append(make_fourier_start(n,K,float(rng.uniform(0.4,1.0)),rng))
    # modes sweep
    ph1_list=(0.0, math.pi/4, -math.pi/4)
    amp2_list=(-0.8,-0.4,0.0,0.4,0.8)
    ph2_list=(0.0, math.pi/2)
    for ph1 in ph1_list:
       for amp2 in amp2_list:
          for ph2 in ph2_list:
             starts.append(make_modes_start(n, ((1,1.0,ph1),(2,amp2,ph2))))
       for amp3 in (-0.5,0.0,0.5):
          starts.append(make_modes_start(n, ((1,1.0,ph1),(3,amp3,0.0))))
    # score starts and top
    scored=[]
    for a in starts:
       s,c=score_and_conv_fft(a,L)
       if math.isfinite(s):
          scored.append((s,a))
    if not scored:
       a=make_sinusoidal(n,1.0,0.0); s,c=score_and_conv_fft(a,L); scored.append((s,a))
    # top count
    top_count = min(10, len(scored))
    used=[]
    for i in range(len(scored)): used.append(False)
    top_indices=[]
    for _ in range(top_count):
       bi=-1; bv=np.inf
       for i in range(len(scored)):
          if not used[i] and scored[i][0] < bv:
             bv=scored[i][0]; bi=i
       if bi <0: break
       used[bi]=True; top_indices.append(bi)
    # add random unused up to top_count? Already top_count top. Maybe add 2 random distinct if len>top_count
    extra_count = min(2, max(0, len(scored)-len(top_indices)))
    for _ in range(extra_count):
       idx=int(rng.integers(0,len(scored)))
       if not used[idx]:
          used[idx]=True; top_indices.append(idx)
    print(f"large search n={n} starts={len(scored)} top={len(top_indices)}", flush=True)
    # run local
    total_walk = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
    walk_budget = total_walk * 0.7
    for idx_top in top_indices:
       if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
       a=scored[idx_top][1]
       remaining=deadline - time.monotonic()
       left_count=max(1, len(top_indices) - top_indices.index(idx_top))? No lambda and index O(n). We'll track loop position with enumerate? No lambda but enumerate is fine. Use `for pos, idx_top in enumerate(top_indices):`. `enumerate` okay.
       left = len(top_indices)-pos
       slice_s = min(300.0, max(5.0, walk_budget / max(1,left)))
       # but if walk_budget was initial, and first slice uses walk_budget/left, total walk_budget. Good.
       a,sc=local_search(a, slice_s, rng, p_init=16.0, lr_init=0.35)
       if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
          best_score=sc; best_seq=a.copy(); print
    # final polish
    if best_seq is not None and (deadline - time.monotonic()) > 30:
       # try high p
       a,sc=local_search(best_seq, min(600.0, deadline-time.monotonic()), rng, p_init=200.0, lr_init=0.25)
       if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
          best_score=sc; best_seq=a.copy(); print
    return best_seq,best_score
```

Now, final propose:
```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    start=time.monotonic()
    deadline=start + max(1.0, float(budget_s) - 5.0)
    # default
    best_seq = make_sinusoidal(1024, 1.0, 0.0)
    best_score, _ = score_and_conv_fft(best_seq)
    print(f"init score={best_score:.6f} n=1024", flush=True)
    # small
    if deadline - time.monotonic() > 30:
       small_budget=min(180.0, max(0.0,(deadline-time.monotonic())*0.2))
       seq,sc=small_n_search(small_budget,rng)
       if seq is not None and math.isfinite(sc) and sc<best_score:
          best_seq=seq; best_score=sc; print
    # base perturbation if available
    try:
       base = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
       if base.size >= 512 and base.size <= 10000:
           base = normalize_pos(base)
           phases = np.linspace(0,2*math.pi,4,endpoint=False)
           base_budget = min(90.0, max(0.0,(deadline-time.monotonic())*0.07))
           for phase in phases:
              if time.monotonic() >= deadline or base_budget <=1: break
              i=np.arange(base.size,dtype=np.float64)
              mod = 1.0 + 0.95*np.sin(2.0*math.pi*i/float(base.size) + float(phase))
              a = normalize_pos(base*mod,1e-12)
              slice_s = min(30.0, max(1.0, base_budget))
              a,sc=local_search(a,slice_s,rng,p_init=16.0,lr_init=0.35)
              base_budget -= slice_s
              if math.isfinite(sc) and sc<best_score:
                 best_seq=a; best_score=sc; print
    except Exception:
       pass
    # large
    if deadline - time.monotonic() > 60:
       large_budget = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
       # if budget huge maybe leave 20s
       seq,sc=large_n_search(large_budget, rng, 4096)
       if seq is not None and math.isfinite(sc) and sc<best_score:
          best_seq=seq; best_score=sc; print
    # final verify and maybe convert
    if best_seq is None:
       best_seq=make_sinusoidal(1024,1.0,0.0); best_score,_=score_and_conv_fft(best_seq)
    # optional final evaluate_sequence if time
    if deadline - time.monotonic() > 3 and best_seq.size < 10000:
       try:
          test_seq=[float(x) for x in best_seq]
          val=float(evaluate_sequence(test_seq))
          if math.isfinite(val) and val < best_score + 1e-7:
              best_score=val
          elif not math.isfinite(val):
              fallback=make_sinusoidal(512,1.0,0.0)
              best_seq=fallback; best_score,_=score_and_conv_fft(fallback)
       except Exception:
          pass
    return [float(x) for x in best_seq]
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math

try:
    from scipy.optimize import differential_evolution
except Exception:
    differential_evolution = None


_DE_N = 1


def de_objective_small(x):
    """Objective for small-n global search.  The length is set via _DE_N."""
    n = _DE_N
    a = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return 1e9
    a = a / s
    c = np.convolve(a, a, "full")
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if m < 0.0 or not math.isfinite(m):
        return 1e9
    return float(2.0 * n * m)


def next_fast_len(m):
    """Smallest power of two >= m."""
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (int(m) - 1).bit_length()


def normalize_pos(a, floor=1e-12):
    """Clip to nonnegative and rescale to sum 1."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return a
    a = np.clip(a, floor, None)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(a.size, 1.0 / float(a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return a / s


def score_conv_fft_with_fa(a, L):
    """Return (score, convolution coefficients, FFT(a))."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return (
            np.inf,
            np.array([], dtype=np.float64),
            np.array([], dtype=np.complex128),
        )

    fa = np.fft.fft(a, n=L)
    c = np.fft.ifft(fa * fa).real
    c = c[: 2 * n - 1]

    S = float(a.sum())
    if S <= 1e-12 or not math.isfinite(S):
        return np.inf, c, fa

    m = float(np.max(c))
    if m < 0.0 or not math.isfinite(m):
        return np.inf, c, fa

    return float(2.0 * n * m / (S * S)), c, fa


def score_and_conv_fft(a, L=None):
    """Convenience wrapper returning (score, convolution)."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf, np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if L is None:
        L = next_fast_len(2 * n - 1)
    s, c, _fa = score_conv_fft_with_fa(a, L)
    return s, c


def a_from_log(loga, floor=1e-12):
    """Convert log-weights to a normalized nonnegative array."""
    loga = np.asarray(loga, dtype=np.float64)
    if loga.size == 0:
        return loga

    m = float(np.max(loga))
    if not math.isfinite(m):
        return np.full(loga.size, 1.0 / float(loga.size), dtype=np.float64)

    with np.errstate(under="ignore", over="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
        a = np.exp(loga - m)

    a = np.where(a < floor, floor, a)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(loga.size, 1.0 / float(loga.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return a / s


def random_fourier_noise(n, rng, sigma, max_k):
    """Centered smooth-ish noise using low Fourier modes."""
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 2 or sigma <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    max_k = int(max_k)
    max_k = min(max_k, n // 2 - 1)
    if max_k <= 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    k = np.arange(1, max_k + 1, dtype=np.int64)
    amp = sigma / np.sqrt(k.astype(np.float64))

    cr = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, max_k)
    sr = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, max_k)
    vals = amp * (cr + 1j * sr)

    spec = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.complex128)
    spec[k] = vals
    spec[-k] = np.conj(vals)

    noise = np.fft.ifft(spec).real
    noise -= float(np.mean(noise))
    return noise


def make_sinusoidal(n, alpha, phase):
    """A basic asymmetric nonnegative start: 1 + alpha*sin(...)."""
    n = int(n)
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = 1.0 + alpha * np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * i / float(n) + phase)
    return normalize_pos(a, 1e-12)


def make_modes_start(n, modes):
    """
    Sequence of the form 1 + sum amp_k * sin(2*pi*k*j/n + phase_k).
    `modes` is an iterable of triples (k, amp, phase).
    """
    n = int(n)
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

    for mode in modes:
        k = int(mode[0])
        amp = float(mode[1])
        phase = float(mode[2])
        if k == 0:
            a += amp
        else:
            a += amp * np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * float(k) * i / float(n) + phase)

    return normalize_pos(a, 1e-12)


def make_fourier_start(n, K, eps, rng):
    """Random band-limited perturbation of a flat sequence."""
    h = random_fourier_noise(n, rng, 1.0, K)
    h -= float(h.mean())
    m = float(np.max(np.abs(h)))
    if m > 0.0 and math.isfinite(m):
        h = h / m
    else:
        h = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    a = 1.0 + eps * h
    return normalize_pos(a, 1e-12)


def local_search(a, max_seconds, rng=None, p_init=16.0, lr_init=0.35, max_p=10000.0):
    """
    Exponentiated-gradient style local search for minimizing
        2n * max_k(a*a)_k / (sum a)^2.

    The update is a smooth max-peak gradient in Fourier convolution form.
    Annealing of p focuses the weight from averaged peaks to true maxima.
    """
    a = normalize_pos(a, 1e-12)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64), np.inf

    L = next_fast_len(2 * n - 1)
    score, c, fa = score_conv_fft_with_fa(a, L)

    if not math.isfinite(score):
        a = make_sinusoidal(n, 1.0, 0.0)
        score, c, fa = score_conv_fft_with_fa(a, L)
        if not math.isfinite(score):
            a = np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
            score, c, fa = score_conv_fft_with_fa(a, L)

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = float(score)
    prints = 0

    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(54321)

    loga = np.log(np.clip(a, 1e-12, 1.0))
    p = float(p_init)
    lr = float(lr_init)
    bad = 0
    it = 0

    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + max(0.1, float(max_seconds))

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        M = float(np.max(c))
        if M <= 1e-300 or not math.isfinite(M):
            break

        x = c / M
        x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)

        with np.errstate(under="ignore", over="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
            w = np.power(x, p)

        ws = float(w.sum())
        if ws <= 1e-300 or not math.isfinite(ws):
            w = np.zeros_like(c)
            w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        else:
            w = w / ws

        fw = np.fft.fft(w, n=L)
        h = np.fft.ifft(fa * np.conj(fw)).real[:n]
        h = np.where(h > 0.0, h, 0.0)

        g = h - float(np.dot(a, h))
        gmax = float(np.max(np.abs(g)))

        if gmax < 1e-14 or not math.isfinite(gmax):
            p = min(max_p, p * 2.0)
            bad += 1

            if bad > 25:
                loga = np.log(np.clip(best_a, 1e-12, 1.0))
                noise = random_fourier_noise(n, rng, 0.5, max(1, n // 16))
                if float(np.max(np.abs(noise))) < 1e-6:
                    noise = rng.normal(0.0, 0.4, n)
                loga = loga + noise

                a = a_from_log(loga, 1e-12)
                loga = np.log(np.clip(a, 1e-12, 1.0))

                score, c, fa = score_conv_fft_with_fa(a, L)
                if math.isfinite(score) and score < best_score:
                    best_score = score
                    best_a = a.copy()

                bad = 0
                lr = 0.6
                p = min(max_p, max(p, 32.0))

            continue

        step = lr * (g / gmax)
        new_log = loga - step
        new_log = new_log - float(np.max(new_log))

        with np.errstate(under="ignore", over="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
            new_a = np.exp(new_log)

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(new_a)):
            lr = max(0.01, lr * 0.5)
            bad += 1
            continue

        new_a = np.where(new_a < 1e-12, 1e-12, new_a)
        snew = float(new_a.sum())
        if snew <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(snew):
            lr = max(0.01, lr * 0.5)
            bad += 1
            continue

        new_a = new_a / snew

        fa_new = np.fft.fft(new_a, n=L)
        c_new = np.fft.ifft(fa_new * fa_new).real[: 2 * n - 1]
        m_new = float(np.max(c_new))
        if m_new < 0.0:
            m_new = 0.0

        S2 = float(new_a.sum())
        if S2 <= 1e-12 or not math.isfinite(S2):
            new_score = np.inf
        else:
            new_score = float(2.0 * n * m_new / (S2 * S2))

        it += 1

        if math.isfinite(new_score) and new_score < score - 1e-10:
            a = new_a
            c = c_new
            fa = fa_new
            score = float(new_score)
            loga = np.log(np.clip(a, 1e-12, 1.0))

            if score < best_score:
                best_score = score
                best_a = a.copy()
                if prints < 3 and score < best_score_estimate_sentinel(0.0, 1e-5):
                    pass
                if prints < 3:
                    print(f"local_search n={n} score={best_score:.8f}", flush=True)
                    prints += 1

            bad = 0
            lr = min(1.0, lr * 1.08)
        else:
            bad += 1
            lr = max(0.01, lr * 0.75)
            p = min(max_p, p * 1.08)

            if bad > 50:
                loga = np.log(np.clip(best_a, 1e-12, 1.0))
                noise = random_fourier_noise(n, rng, 0.6, max(1, n // 16))
                if float(np.max(np.abs(noise))) < 1e-6:
                    noise = rng.normal(0.0, 0.5, n)
                loga = loga + noise

                a = a_from_log(loga, 1e-12)
                loga = np.log(np.clip(a, 1e-12, 1.0))

                score, c, fa = score_conv_fft_with_fa(a, L)
                if math.isfinite(score) and score < best_score:
                    best_score = score
                    best_a = a.copy()

                bad = 0
                lr = 0.7
                p = min(max_p, max(p, 32.0))

        if it % 200 == 0:
            p = min(max_p, p * 1.05)

    return best_a, float(best_score)


def best_score_estimate_sentinel(_a, _b):
    """No-op helper kept for compatibility with local_search print guard."""
    return 1e18


def small_n_search(max_seconds, rng):
    """Search for short sequences using random restarts + differential evolution."""
    global _DE_N

    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + max(1.0, float(max_seconds))

    best_seq = None
    best_score = np.inf

    if max_seconds < 30:
        n_values = (2, 3, 4, 5, 6)
    elif max_seconds < 90:
        n_values = (2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8, 9, 10)
    else:
        n_values = (2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8, 9, 10, 12, 14, 16, 18, 20)

    for n in n_values:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break

        # A few cheap structured/unstructured restarts.
        n_restart = 3 if n <= 6 else 2
        for r in range(n_restart):
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                break

            if r == 0:
                a = normalize_pos(0.1 + rng.random(n))
            elif r == 1:
                a = make_sinusoidal(n, 1.0, float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi)))
            else:
                a = make_fourier_start(n, max(1, n // 2), float(rng.uniform(0.4, 1.0)), rng)

            rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
            if rem < 0.5:
                break

            slice_s = min(2.0, max(0.3, rem * 0.5))
            a, sc = local_search(a, slice_s, rng, p_init=8.0, lr_init=0.6)

            if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_seq = a.copy()
                print(f"small_n_search n={n} score={best_score:.8f}", flush=True)

        # Differential evolution, if available.
        if differential_evolution is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 5.0:
            _DE_N = n
            bounds = []
            for _i in range(n):
                bounds.append((0.0, 1.0))

            if n <= 6:
                maxiter = 200
                popsize = 20
            elif n <= 10:
                maxiter = 120
                popsize = 15
            else:
                maxiter = 70
                popsize = 12

            seed_int = int(rng.integers(0, 2**31 - 1))
            res = None

            try:
                res = differential_evolution(
                    de_objective_small,
                    bounds,
                    maxiter=maxiter,
                    popsize=popsize,
                    tol=1e-6,
                    seed=seed_int,
                    polish=False,
                    updating="inplace",
                    workers=1,
                )
            except TypeError:
                try:
                    res = differential_evolution(
                        de_objective_small,
                        bounds,
                        maxiter=maxiter,
                        popsize=popsize,
                        tol=1e-6,
                        seed=seed_int,
                        polish=False,
                    )
                except Exception:
                    res = None
            except Exception:
                res = None

            if res is not None:
                x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
                a = normalize_pos(x, 1e-12)

                rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
                if rem > 0.5:
                    slice_s = min(4.0, max(0.5, rem * 0.5))
                    a, sc = local_search(a, slice_s, rng, p_init=8.0, lr_init=0.6)

                    if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                        best_score = sc
                        best_seq = a.copy()
                        print(f"small_n_search DE n={n} score={best_score:.8f}", flush=True)

    return best_seq, float(best_score)


def large_n_search(max_seconds, rng, n_pref=0):
    """Multi-start local search for long sequences."""
    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + max(1.0, float(max_seconds))

    if n_pref > 0:
        n = int(n_pref)
    elif max_seconds >= 800:
        n = 4096
    elif max_seconds >= 350:
        n = 2048
    else:
        n = 1024

    n = min(max(16, n), 8192)
    L = next_fast_len(2 * n - 1)

    starts = []

    # Basic sinusoidal starts with scalable asymmetry.
    phases = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi, 8, endpoint=False)
    for phase in phases:
        starts.append(make_sinusoidal(n, 1.0, float(phase)))

    for phase in phases[::2]:
        starts.append(make_sinusoidal(n, 0.8, float(phase)))

    # Random band-limited starts.
    for K in (4, 16, 64, 256):
        for _r in range(2):
            starts.append(
                make_fourier_start(n, K, float(rng.uniform(0.4, 1.0)), rng)
            )

    # A small deterministic sweep of two/three low modes often produces strong starts.
    ph1_list = (0.0, math.pi / 4.0, -math.pi / 4.0)
    amp2_list = (-0.8, -0.4, 0.0, 0.4, 0.8)
    ph2_list = (0.0, math.pi / 2.0)

    for ph1 in ph1_list:
        for amp2 in amp2_list:
            for ph2 in ph2_list:
                starts.append(
                    make_modes_start(n, ((1, 1.0, ph1), (2, amp2, ph2)))
                )

        for amp3 in (-0.5, 0.0, 0.5):
            starts.append(
                make_modes_start(n, ((1, 1.0, ph1), (3, amp3, 0.0)))
            )

    scored = []
    for a in starts:
        s, _c = score_and_conv_fft(a, L)
        if math.isfinite(s):
            scored.append((s, a))

    if not scored:
        a = make_sinusoidal(n, 1.0, 0.0)
        s, _c = score_and_conv_fft(a, L)
        scored.append((s, a))

    # Manual top-K selection without lambdas.
    top_count = min(10, len(scored))
    used = []
    for _i in range(len(scored)):
        used.append(False)

    top_indices = []
    for _tk in range(top_count):
        bi = -1
        bv = np.inf
        for i in range(len(scored)):
            if not used[i] and scored[i][0] < bv:
                bv = scored[i][0]
                bi = i
        if bi < 0:
            break
        used[bi] = True
        top_indices.append(bi)

    # Add a couple of non-top starts to avoid overcommitment to one basin.
    extra_count = min(2, max(0, len(scored) - len(top_indices)))
    for _ek in range(extra_count):
        idx = int(rng.integers(0, len(scored)))
        if not used[idx]:
            used[idx] = True
            top_indices.append(idx)

    best_seq = None
    best_score = np.inf

    print(f"large_n_search n={n} valid_starts={len(scored)} run_top={len(top_indices)}", flush=True)

    total_walk = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
    walk_budget = 0.7 * total_walk

    for pos, idx_top in enumerate(top_indices):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break

        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 5.0:
            break

        a = scored[idx_top][1]
        left = max(1, len(top_indices) - pos)
        slice_s = min(300.0, max(5.0, walk_budget / float(left)))
        slice_s = min(slice_s, rem - 1.0)
        if slice_s < 1.0:
            slice_s = 1.0

        a, sc = local_search(a, slice_s, rng, p_init=16.0, lr_init=0.35)

        if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_seq = a.copy()
            print(f"large_n_search n={n} current_best={best_score:.8f}", flush=True)

    # Final polish on the best found so far.
    if best_seq is not None and (deadline - time.monotonic()) > 30.0:
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        a, sc = local_search(
            best_seq,
            min(900.0, rem - 1.0),
            rng,
            p_init=200.0,
            lr_init=0.25,
        )
        if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_seq = a.copy()
            print(f"large_n_search polished n={n} best={best_score:.8f}", flush=True)

    return best_seq, float(best_score)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    """
    Multi-start search for a nonnegative step-function coefficient sequence
    minimizing 2n * max conv(a,a) / (sum a)^2.

    Strategy:
      1. Keep a valid sinusoidal fallback.
      2. Search short sequences globally (DE + local polish); small lengths can
         be surprisingly good.
      3. If available, perturb any provided previous best sequence.
      4. Run the main long-sequence multi-start exponentiated-gradient search.
    """
    try:
        bs = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        bs = 1800.0

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + max(1.0, bs - 5.0)

    # Guaranteed valid baseline.  This already beats the all-ones score 2.
    best_seq = make_sinusoidal(1024, 1.0, 0.0)
    best_score, _ = score_and_conv_fft(best_seq)
    print(f"initial fallback score={best_score:.8f} n=1024", flush=True)

    # Short-length global search.
    if deadline - time.monotonic() > 30.0:
        small_budget = min(180.0, max(0.0, (deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.20))
        seq, sc = small_n_search(small_budget, rng)
        if seq is not None and math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_seq = seq
            best_score = sc
            print(f"global best after small-n: {best_score:.8f}", flush=True)

    # If a previous sequence is exposed, try strongly modulating it as well.
    try:
        base = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
        if base.size >= 512 and base.size <= 10000:
            base = normalize_pos(base, 1e-12)
            phases = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi, 4, endpoint=False)
            base_budget = min(90.0, max(0.0, (deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.07))

            for phase in phases:
                if base_budget <= 1.5 or time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                    break

                i = np.arange(base.size, dtype=np.float64)
                mod = 1.0 + 0.95 * np.sin(
                    2.0 * math.pi * i / float(base.size) + float(phase)
                )
                a = normalize_pos(base * mod, 1e-12)

                rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
                slice_s = min(30.0, max(1.0, base_budget))
                slice_s = min(slice_s, rem - 1.0)
                if slice_s < 1.0:
                    continue

                a, sc = local_search(a, slice_s, rng, p_init=16.0, lr_init=0.35)
                base_budget -= slice_s

                if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                    best_seq = a
                    best_score = sc
                    print(
                        f"base-modulated best={best_score:.8f} n={base.size}",
                        flush=True,
                    )
    except Exception:
        pass

    # Main long-sequence search.
    if deadline - time.monotonic() > 60.0:
        large_budget = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 5.0)
        seq, sc = large_n_search(large_budget, rng, 4096)
        if seq is not None and math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_seq = seq
            best_score = sc
            print(f"global best after large-n: {best_score:.8f}", flush=True)

    if best_seq is None:
        best_seq = make_sinusoidal(1024, 1.0, 0.0)
        best_score, _ = score_and_conv_fft(best_seq)

    # Final validation against the official evaluator, when cheap enough.
    if deadline - time.monotonic() > 3.0 and best_seq.size < 10000:
        try:
            test_seq = [float(x) for x in best_seq]
            val = float(evaluate_sequence(test_seq))
            if math.isfinite(val):
                best_score = min(best_score, val)
            else:
                fallback = make_sinusoidal(512, 1.0, 0.0)
                best_seq = fallback
                best_score, _ = score_and_conv_fft(fallback)
        except Exception:
            pass

    return [float(x) for x in best_seq]
```
